Normalize metadata early for consistent handling throughout the function\n        meta_pairs = _normalise_meta(meta)\n        meta_payload = {key: value for key, value in meta_pairs}\n\n        # Auto-detect agent ID if not provided\n        if agent_id is None:\n            agent_identity = server_module.get_agent_identity()\n            if agent_identity:\n                agent_id = await agent_identity.get_or_create_agent_id()\n            else:\n                agent_id = \"Scribe\"  # "chunk_index": 0, "content": "# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-\n\"\"\"\nConfigurable reminder engine for Scribe MCP - Backwards Compatibility Shim.\n\nThis module provides a drop-in replacement for the original reminders.py,\nrouting all calls to the new localization-based reminder system.\n\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport asyncio\nfrom datetime import datetime, timezone\nfrom pathlib import Path\nfrom typing import Any, Dict, List, Optional, Sequence\n\n# Import the new reminder engine\nfrom scribe_mcp.utils.reminder_validator import validate_and_load_engine\nfrom scribe_mcp.utils.reminder_engine import ReminderEngine, ReminderContext as NewReminderContext\n\n# Global engine instance (singleton pattern)\n_reminder_engine: Optional[ReminderEngine] = None\n\ndef _get_engine() -> ReminderEngine:\n    \"\"\"Get or create the reminder engine instance.\"\"\"\n    global _reminder_engine\n    if _reminder_engine is None:\n        _reminder_engine = validate_and_load_engine()\n    return _reminder_engine\n\n\ndef reset_reminder_cooldowns(*, project_root: str, agent_id: Optional[str] = None) -> int:\n    \"\"\"Clear reminder cooldown timestamps for a repo/project (and optionally agent).\"\"\"\n    engine = _get_engine()\n    return engine.reset_cooldowns(project_root=project_root, agent_id=agent_id)\n\n\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Legacy Compatibility API\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nasync def get_reminders(\n    project: Dict[str, Any],\n    *,\n    tool_name: str,\n    state: Optional[object] = None,\n    agent_id: Optional[str] = None,\n    variables: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = None,\n) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Legacy compatibility wrapper for the original get_reminders function.\n\n    This maintains the exact same interface as the original while using\n    the new reminder engine under the hood.\n    \"\"\"\n    if not project:\n        return []\n\n    # Build the new reminder context from the old format\n    context = await _build_legacy_context(\n        project,\n        tool_name,\n        state,\n        agent_id=agent_id,\n        variables=variables,\n    )\n\n    # Use the new engine\n    engine = _get_engine()\n    reminder_instances = await engine.generate_reminders(context)\n\n    # Convert to the old format\n    return engine.to_dict_list(reminder_instances)\n\n\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Context Building Functions\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nasync def _build_legacy_context(\n    project: Dict[str, Any],\n    tool_name: str,\n    state: Optional[object],\n    *,\n    agent_id: Optional[str] = None,\n    variables: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = None,\n) -> NewReminderContext:\n    \"\"\"Convert legacy project/state format to new ReminderContext.\"\"\"\n\n    # Extract project information\n    project_name = project.get(\"name\")\n    project_root = project.get(\"root\")\n    log_path = Path(project.get(\"progress_log\", \"\"))\n\n    # Read log information (similar to original _build_context)\n    last_log_time: Optional[datetime] = None\n    total_entries = 0\n    minutes_since_log: Optional[float] = None\n\n    try:\n        from scribe_mcp.utils.logs import read_all_lines, parse_log_line\n        lines = await read_all_lines(log_path)\n\n        for line in lines:\n            if parse_log_line(line):\n                total_entries += 1\n\n        for line in reversed(lines):\n            parsed = parse_log_line(line)\n            if not parsed:\n                continue\n            ts_str = parsed.get(\"ts\")\n            if ts_str:\n                try:\n                    from scribe_mcp.utils.time import parse_utc, utcnow\n                    last_log_time = parse_utc(ts_str)\n                    delta = utcnow() - last_log_time\n                    minutes_since_log = delta.total_seconds() / 60\n                    break\n                except Exception:\n                    pass\n    except Exception:\n        # If we can't read logs, use defaults\n        pass\n\n    # Extract docs information\n    docs_status = {}\n    docs_changed = []\n\n    try:\n        if state:\n            # Try to get docs information from state\n            if hasattr(state, 'projects') and project_name in state.projects:\n                state_project = state.projects[project_name]\n                docs_status = state_project.get(\"docs_status\", {})\n                docs_changed = state_project.get(\"docs_changed\", [])\n\n        # Fall back to checking docs directly\n        if not docs_status and \"docs\" in project:\n            docs = project[\"docs\"] or {}\n            for doc_type, doc_path in docs.items():\n                if doc_type == \"progress_log\":\n                    continue\n                path = Path(doc_path)\n                if not path.exists():\n                    docs_status[doc_type] = \"missing\"\n                else:\n                    try:\n                        content = await asyncio.to_thread(path.read_text, encoding=\"utf-8\")\n                        # Simple completion check\n                        if \"{{\" in content and \"}}\" in content:\n                            docs_status[doc_type] = \"incomplete\"\n                        elif len(content.strip()) < 400:\n                            docs_status[doc_type] = \"incomplete\"\n                        else:\n                            docs_status[doc_type] = \"complete\"\n                    except Exception:\n                        docs_status[doc_type] = \"missing\"\n\n    except Exception:\n        # If we can't check docs, use empty status\n        pass\n\n    # Get current phase\n    current_phase = None\n    try:\n        phase_plan_path = project.get(\"docs\", {}).get(\"phase_plan\")\n        if phase_plan_path:\n            phase_path = Path(phase_plan_path)\n            if phase_path.exists():\n                import re\n                content = await asyncio.to_thread(phase_path.read_text, encoding=\"utf-8\")\n                match = re.search(r\"##\\s+Phase\\s+(.+?)\\s*\\(In Progress\\)\", content)\n                if match:\n                    current_phase = match.group(1).strip()\n    except Exception:\n        pass\n\n    # Get session information\n    session_age_minutes = None\n    try:\n        if state and hasattr(state, 'session_started_at') and state.session_started_at:\n            from scribe_mcp.utils.time import parse_utc, utcnow\n            start_dt = parse_utc(state.session_started_at)\n            if start_dt:\n                age_delta = utcnow() - start_dt\n                session_age_minutes = age_delta.total_seconds() / 60\n    except Exception:\n        pass\n\n    return NewReminderContext(\n        tool_name=tool_name,\n        project_name=project_name,\n        project_root=str(project_root) if project_root else None,\n        agent_id=agent_id,\n        total_entries=total_entries,\n        minutes_since_log=minutes_since_log,\n        last_log_time=last_log_time,\n        docs_status=docs_status,\n        docs_changed=docs_changed,\n        current_phase=current_phase,\n", "line_end": 200, "line_start": 1}], "frontmatter": {}, "chunk_index": 0, "content": "\"\"\"Advanced reminder engine with localization and intelligent selection.\n\nThis module provides a sophisticated reminder system that:\n- Loads reminders from configurable JSON files\n- Supports multiple languages with fallbacks\n- Implements intelligent reminder selection and deduplication\n- Provides progressive teaching with cooldown periods\n- Uses variable substitution for dynamic content\n\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport hashlib\nimport json\nimport os\nimport time\nfrom dataclasses import dataclass, field\nfrom pathlib import Path\nfrom typing import Any, Dict, List, Optional, Set, Tuple\nfrom datetime import datetime, timezone, timedelta\n\nfrom scribe_mcp.config.settings import settings\n\n\n@dataclass\nclass ReminderInstance:\n    \"\"\"A single reminder instance with metadata.\"\"\"\n    key: str\n    level: str\n    emoji: str\n    message: str\n    context: Optional[str] = None\n    category: str = \"general\"\n    score: int = 3\n    variables: Dict[str, Any] = field(default_factory=dict)\n    tools_suppressed: List[str] = field(default_factory=list)\n    cooldown_minutes: int = 0\n    last_shown: Optional[datetime] = None\n\n\n@dataclass\nclass ReminderContext:\n    \"\"\"Context for reminder generation.\"\"\"\n    tool_name: str\n    project_name: Optional[str]\n    project_root: Optional[str]\n    agent_id: Optional[str]\n    total_entries: int\n    minutes_since_log: Optional[float]\n    last_log_time: Optional[datetime]\n    docs_status: Dict[str, str]\n    docs_changed: List[str]\n    current_phase: Optional[str]\n    session_age_minutes: Optional[float]\n    variables: Dict[str, Any] = field(default_factory=dict)\n\n\n@dataclass\nclass ReminderHistory:\n    \"\"\"Tracks recently shown reminders for deduplication.\"\"\"\n    reminder_hashes: Dict[str, datetime] = field(default_factory=dict)\n    teaching_sessions: Dict[str, int] = field(default_factory=dict)\n    last_cleanup: datetime = field(default_factory=lambda: datetime.now(timezone.utc))\n\n\nclass ReminderEngine:\n    \"\"\"Advanced reminder engine with localization and intelligent selection.\"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self, config_path: Optional[str] = None):\n        self.config_path = config_path or \"config/reminder_config.json\"\n        self.reminders_path: Optional[str] = None\n        self.rules_path: Optional[str] = None\n\n        self.config: Dict[str, Any] = {}\n        self.reminders: Dict[str, Any] = {}\n        self.rules: Dict[str, Any] = {}\n        self.variables: Dict[str, Any] = {}\n        self.formatting: Dict[str, Any] = {}\n\n        self.language = \"en-US\"\n        self.fallback_language = \"en-US\"\n\n        self.history = ReminderHistory()\n        self._cooldown_cache_path: Optional[Path] = None\n        self._cooldown_cache_dirty = False\n\n        self._load_configuration()\n\n    def _load_configuration(self) -> None:\n        \"\"\"Load all configuration files.\"\"\"\n        try:\n            config_file = Path(self.config_path)\n            if config_file.exists():\n                self.config = json.loads(config_file.read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\"))\n                self.language = self.config.get(\"language\", \"en-US\")\n                self.fallback_language = self.config.get(\"fallback_language\", \"en-US\")\n\n                base_path = config_file.parent\n                self.reminders_path = base_path / self.config.get(\"reminder_paths\", {}).get(\"templates\", \"reminders\")\n                self.rules_path = base_path / self.config.get(\"reminder_paths\", {}).get(\"rules\", \"reminder_rules.json\")\n\n            self._load_reminders()\n            self._load_rules()\n            self._configure_cooldown_cache()\n            self._load_cooldown_cache()\n\n        except Exception as e:\n            print(f\"Warning: Failed to load reminder configuration: {e}\")\n            self._load_fallback_reminders()\n\n    def _load_reminders(self) -> None:\n        \"\"\"Load reminder templates for current language.\"\"\"\n        if not self.reminders_path:\n            return\n\n        # Try to load preferred language\n        lang_file = self.reminders_path / f\"{self.language}.json\"\n        if lang_file.exists():\n            self.reminders = json.loads(lang_file.read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\"))\n            self.variables = self.reminders.get(\"variables\", {})\n            self.formatting = self.reminders.get(\"formatting\", {})\n            return\n\n        # Fallback to default language\n        fallback_file = self.reminders_path / f\"{self.fallback_language}.json\"\n        if fallback_file.exists():\n            self.reminders = json.loads(fallback_file.read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\"))\n            self.variables = self.reminders.get(\"variables\", {})\n            self.formatting = self.reminders.get(\"formatting\", {})\n\n    def _load_rules(self) -> None:\n        \"\"\"Load reminder selection rules.\"\"\"\n        if not self.rules_path or not self.rules_path.exists():\n            return\n\n        self.rules = json.loads(self.rules_path.read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\"))\n\n    def _load_fallback_reminders(self) -> None:\n        \"\"\"Load minimal fallback reminders.\"\"\"\n        self.reminders = {\n            \"reminders\": {\n                \"logging\": {\n                    \"no_logs_yet\": {\n                        \"level\": \"info\",\n                        \"emoji\": \"\ud83d\udcdd\",\n                        \"template\": \"No progress logs yet. Use append_entry to start the audit trail.\",\n                        \"category\": \"logging\"\n                    }\n                },\n                \"context\": {\n                    \"project_context\": {\n                        \"level\": \"info\",\n                        \"emoji\": \"\ud83c\udfaf\",\n                        \"template\": \"Project: {project_name}\",\n                        \"category\": \"context\"\n                    }\n                }\n            }\n        }\n        self.config = {\n            \"behavior\": {\"max_reminders_per_call\": 2},\n            \"selection\": {\"priority_order\": [\"urgent\", \"warning\", \"info\"]}\n        }\n\n    def _cleanup_history(self) -> None:\n        \"\"\"Clean up old reminder history.\"\"\"\n        now = datetime.now(timezone.utc)\n        cleanup_after_hours = self.config.get(\"tracking\", {}).get(\"cleanup_after_hours\", 24)\n        cutoff = now - timedelta(hours=cleanup_after_hours)\n\n        # Remove old reminder hashes\n        self.history.reminder_hashes = {\n            h: t for h, t in self.history.reminder_hashes.items()\n            if t > cutoff\n        }\n\n        # Remove old teaching sessions\n        self.history.teaching_sessions = {\n            k: v for k, v in self.history.teaching_sessions.items()\n            if v > 0  # Sessions reset when count reaches 0\n        }\n\n        self.history.last_cleanup = now\n\n        # Keep the persisted cooldown cache bounded as well.\n        self._cleanup_cooldown_cache()\n        self._save_cooldown_cache()\n\n    def _configure_cooldown_cache(self) -> None:\n        override = os.environ.get(\"SCRIBE_REMINDER_CACHE_PATH\")\n        if override:\n            self._cooldown_cache_path = Path(override).expanduser()\n            return\n\n        behavior = self.config.get(\"behavior\", {}) if isinstance(self.config, dict) else {}\n        path_raw = behavior.get(\"cooldown_cache_path\", \"data/reminder_cooldowns.json\")\n        path_obj = Path(path_raw).expanduser()\n        if not path_obj.is_absolute():\n            path_obj = (settings.project_root / path_obj).resolve()\n        self._cooldown_cache_path = path_obj\n", "line_end": 200, "line_start": 1}], "frontmatter": {}, "frontmatter_byte_count": 0, "frontmatter_line_count": 0, "frontmatter_raw": "", "has_frontmatter": false, "mode": "chunk", "ok": true, "reminders": [{"category": "context", "context": "Entries:    Last: ", "emoji": "\ud83c\udfaf", "level": "info", "message": "Project: scribe_sentinel_concurrency_v1", "score": 3, "tone": "neutral"}], "scan": {"absolute_path": "/home/austin/projects/MCP_SPINE/scribe_mcp/utils/reminder_engine.py", "byte_size": 23052, "encoding": "utf-8", "estimated_chunk_count": 3, "line_count": 559, "newline_type": "LF", "repo_relative_path": "utils/reminder_engine.py", "sha256": "952cac2de13c77dc7e729f72d71c740b4ad8e3f608019ab0d3bc76351f25010d"}}; timestamp=2026-01-03T06:50:35.877004Z; tool=read_file; priority=medium; log_type=tool_logs; content_type=log

7322, "chunk_index": 1, "content": "\n    def _load_cooldown_cache(self) -> None:\n        behavior = self.config.get(\"behavior\", {}) if isinstance(self.config, dict) else {}\n        enabled = bool(behavior.get(\"persist_cooldowns\", True))\n        if not enabled or not self._cooldown_cache_path:\n            return\n\n        try:\n            path = self._cooldown_cache_path\n            if not path.exists():\n                return\n            raw = json.loads(path.read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\"))\n            entries = raw.get(\"entries\", raw) if isinstance(raw, dict) else {}\n            if not isinstance(entries, dict):\n                return\n\n            loaded: Dict[str, datetime] = {}\n            for key, ts in entries.items():\n                if not isinstance(key, str) or not isinstance(ts, str):\n                    continue\n                try:\n                    dt = datetime.fromisoformat(ts)\n                    if dt.tzinfo is None:\n                        dt = dt.replace(tzinfo=timezone.utc)\n                    else:\n                        dt = dt.astimezone(timezone.utc)\n                    loaded[key] = dt\n                except ValueError:\n                    continue\n\n            if loaded:\n                self.history.reminder_hashes.update(loaded)\n        except Exception:\n            return\n\n    def _save_cooldown_cache(self) -> None:\n        behavior = self.config.get(\"behavior\", {}) if isinstance(self.config, dict) else {}\n        enabled = bool(behavior.get(\"persist_cooldowns\", True))\n        if not enabled or not self._cooldown_cache_path or not self._cooldown_cache_dirty:\n            return\n\n        try:\n            path = self._cooldown_cache_path\n            path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n            payload = {\n                \"version\": 1,\n                \"written_at\": datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat(),\n                \"entries\": {k: v.isoformat() for k, v in self.history.reminder_hashes.items()},\n            }\n            tmp = path.with_suffix(path.suffix + \".tmp\")\n            tmp.write_text(json.dumps(payload, sort_keys=True), encoding=\"utf-8\")\n            tmp.replace(path)\n            self._cooldown_cache_dirty = False\n        except Exception:\n            return\n\n    def _cleanup_cooldown_cache(self) -> None:\n        behavior = self.config.get(\"behavior\", {}) if isinstance(self.config, dict) else {}\n        retention_minutes = int(behavior.get(\"cooldown_cache_retention_minutes\", 24 * 60))\n        cutoff = datetime.now(timezone.utc) - timedelta(minutes=max(1, retention_minutes))\n        before = len(self.history.reminder_hashes)\n        self.history.reminder_hashes = {\n            k: v for k, v in self.history.reminder_hashes.items()\n            if v >= cutoff\n        }\n        if len(self.history.reminder_hashes) != before:\n            self._cooldown_cache_dirty = True\n\n    def reset_cooldowns(self, *, project_root: str, agent_id: Optional[str] = None) -> int:\n        prefix = f\"{project_root} \"\n        if agent_id:\n            prefix = f\"{project_root} {agent_id} \"\n\n        keys = [k for k in self.history.reminder_hashes.keys() if k.startswith(prefix)]\n        for key in keys:\n            self.history.reminder_hashes.pop(key, None)\n        if keys:\n            self._cooldown_cache_dirty = True\n            self._save_cooldown_cache()\n        return len(keys)\n\n    def _get_reminder_hash(self, reminder_key: str, variables: Dict[str, Any]) -> str:\n        \"\"\"Generate a stable fingerprint for reminder cooldown checks.\"\"\"\n        project_root = str(variables.get(\"project_root\") or \"\")\n        agent_id = str(variables.get(\"agent_id\") or \"\")\n        tool_name = str(variables.get(\"tool_name\") or \"\")\n        return f\"{project_root} {agent_id} {tool_name} {reminder_key}\"\n\n    def _should_show_reminder(self, reminder: ReminderInstance, context: ReminderContext) -> bool:\n        \"\"\"Check if reminder should be shown based on rules.\"\"\"\n\n        # Tool suppression\n        if context.tool_name in reminder.tools_suppressed:\n            return False\n\n        # Cooldown check\n        cooldown_minutes = reminder.cooldown_minutes\n        if cooldown_minutes <= 0 and reminder.category == \"teaching\":\n            cooldown_minutes = int(self.config.get(\"behavior\", {}).get(\"default_teaching_cooldown_minutes\", 10))\n\n        if cooldown_minutes > 0:\n            reminder_hash = self._get_reminder_hash(reminder.key, reminder.variables)\n            last_shown = self.history.reminder_hashes.get(reminder_hash)\n            if last_shown:\n                cooldown_cutoff = datetime.now(timezone.utc) - timedelta(minutes=cooldown_minutes)\n                if last_shown > cooldown_cutoff:\n                    return False\n\n        # Teaching session limits\n        if reminder.category == \"teaching\":\n            session_key = f\"{context.tool_name}:{reminder.key}\"\n            sessions_used = self.history.teaching_sessions.get(session_key, 0)\n            max_sessions = self.config.get(\"behavior\", {}).get(\"max_teaching_reminders_per_session\", 3)\n            if sessions_used >= max_sessions:\n                return False\n\n        return True\n\n    def _format_reminder(self, reminder: ReminderInstance, use_short: bool = True) -> ReminderInstance:\n        \"\"\"Apply variable substitution and formatting to reminder.\"\"\"\n        # Choose template\n        template_key = \"short_template\" if use_short and \"short_template\" in reminder.variables else \"template\"\n        template = reminder.variables.get(template_key, reminder.message)\n\n        # Variable substitution\n        try:\n            formatted_message = template.format(**reminder.variables)\n            if reminder.context:\n                formatted_context = reminder.context.format(**reminder.variables)\n                reminder.context = formatted_context\n        except KeyError as e:\n            # Fallback to original template if variable missing\n            formatted_message = reminder.message\n\n        reminder.message = formatted_message\n        return reminder\n\n    def _evaluate_condition(self, condition: str, context: ReminderContext) -> bool:\n        \"\"\"Evaluate a condition string against context.\"\"\"\n        # Simple condition evaluation (can be extended)\n        if condition == \"no_log_entries\":\n            return context.total_entries == 0\n        elif condition.startswith(\"minutes_since_log > \"):\n            threshold = int(condition.split()[-1])\n            return (context.minutes_since_log or 0) > threshold\n        elif condition == \"docs_missing\":\n            return any(status == \"missing\" for status in context.docs_status.values())\n        elif condition.startswith(\"tool=\"):\n            return context.tool_name == condition.split(\"=\")[1]\n        elif condition.startswith(\"action=\"):\n            return context.variables.get(\"action\") == condition.split(\"=\")[1]\n        elif condition.startswith(\"scaffold=\"):\n            expected = condition.split(\"=\")[1].strip().lower()\n            actual = context.variables.get(\"scaffold\")\n            if isinstance(actual, bool):\n                actual_value = \"true\" if actual else \"false\"\n            else:\n                actual_value = str(actual).strip().lower()\n            return actual_value == expected\n        elif condition == \"always\":\n            return True\n\n        return False\n\n    def _build_variables(self, context: ReminderContext) -> Dict[str, Any]:\n        \"\"\"Build variable dictionary for template substitution.\"\"\"\n        now_utc = datetime.now(timezone.utc)\n        date_format = self.formatting.get(\"date_format\", \"%Y-%m-%d %H:%M UTC\")\n\n        variables = {\n            \"project_name\": context.project_name or \"No project\",\n            \"project_root\": context.project_root or \"\",\n            \"agent_id\": context.agent_id or \"\",\n            \"tool_name\": context.tool_name,\n            \"total_entries\": context.total_entries,\n            \"minutes\": int(context.minutes_since_log or 0),\n            \"hours\": int((context.minutes_since_log or 0) / 60),\n            \"days\": int((context.minutes_since_log or 0) / 1440),\n            \"now_utc\": now_utc.strftime(date_format),\n            \"now_iso_utc\": now_utc.isoformat(),\n            \"date_utc\": now_utc.strftime(\"%Y-%m-%d\"),\n            \"time_utc\": now_utc.strftime(\"%H:%M:%S UTC\"),\n        }\n\n        # Time formatting\n        if context.last_log_time:\n            variables[\"last_log\"] = context.last_log_time.strftime(\n                date_format\n            )\n        else:\n            variables[\"last_log\"] = \"no logs yet\"\n\n        # Session info\n        if context.session_age_minutes is not None:\n            variables[\"session_age\"] = f\"{context.session_age_minutes:.1f} min\"\n        else:\n            variables[\"session_age\"] = \"\"\n\n        # Phase info\n        if context.current_phase:\n", "line_end": 400, "line_start": 201}], "frontmatter": {}, "frontmatter_byte_count": 0, "chunk_index": 0, "content": "\"\"\"Tool for appending structured entries to the progress log.\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport hashlib\nimport json\nimport uuid\nfrom collections import defaultdict, deque\nfrom datetime import datetime, timezone\nfrom pathlib import Path\nfrom typing import Any, Dict, Optional, Tuple, List, Union\nimport time\n\nimport asyncio\n\nfrom scribe_mcp import server as server_module\nfrom scribe_mcp.config.repo_config import RepoDiscovery\nfrom scribe_mcp.config.settings import settings\nfrom scribe_mcp.server import app\nfrom         self.formatting = self.reminders.get(\\\"formatting\\\", {})\\n\\n    def _load_rules(self) -> None:\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"Load reminder selection rules.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        if not "{\"chunks\": [{\"byte_end\": 6979, \"byte_start\": 0, \"chunk_index\": 0, \"content\": \"# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-\\n\\\"\\\"\\\"\\nConfigurable reminder engine for Scribe MCP - Backwards Compatibility Shim.\\n\\nThis module provides a drop-in replacement for the original reminders.py,\\nrouting all calls to the new localization-based reminder system.\\n\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\nfrom __future__ import annotations\\n\\nimport asyncio\\nfrom datetime import datetime, timezone\\nfrom pathlib import Path\\nfrom typing import Any, Dict, List, Optional, Sequence\\n\\n# Import the new reminder engine\\nfrom scribe_mcp.utils.reminder_validator import validate_and_load_engine\\nfrom scribe_mcp.utils.reminder_engine import ReminderEngine, ReminderContext as NewReminderContext\\n\\n# Global engine instance (singleton pattern)\\n_reminder_engine: Optional[ReminderEngine] = None\\n\\ndef _get_engine() -> ReminderEngine:\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Get or create the reminder engine instance.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    global _reminder_engine\\n    if _reminder_engine is None:\\n        _reminder_engine = validate_and_load_engine()\\n    return _reminder_engine\\n\\n\\ndef reset_reminder_cooldowns(*, project_root: str, agent_id: Optional[str] = None) -> int:\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Clear reminder cooldown timestamps for a repo/project (and optionally agent).\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    engine = _get_engine()\\n    return engine.reset_cooldowns(project_root=project_root, agent_id=agent_id)\\n\\n\\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\\n# Legacy Compatibility API\\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\\n\\nasync def get_reminders(\\n    project: Dict[str, Any],\\n    *,\\n    tool_name: str,\\n    state: Optional[object] = None,\\n    agent_id: Optional[str] = None,\\n    variables: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = None,\\n) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    Legacy compatibility wrapper for the original get_reminders function.\\n\\n    This maintains the exact same interface as the original while using\\n    the new reminder engine under the hood.\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    if not project:\\n        return []\\n\\n    # Build the new reminder context from the old format\\n    context = await _build_legacy_context(\\n        project,\\n        tool_name,\\n        state,\\n        agent_id=agent_id,\\n        variables=variables,\\n    )\\n\\n    # Use the new engine\\n    engine = _get_engine()\\n    reminder_instances = await engine.generate_reminders(context)\\n\\n    # Convert to the old format\\n    return engine.to_dict_list(reminder_instances)\\n\\n\\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\\n# Context Building Functions\\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\\n\\nasync def _build_legacy_context(\\n    project: Dict[str, Any],\\n    tool_name: str,\\n    state: Optional[object],\\n    *,\\n    agent_id: Optional[str] = None,\\n    variables: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = None,\\n) -> NewReminderContext:\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Convert legacy project/state format to new ReminderContext.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\n    # Extract project information\\n    project_name = project.get(\\\"name\\\")\\n    project_root = project.get(\\\"root\\\")\\n    log_path = Path(project.get(\\\"progress_log\\\", \\\"\\\"))\\n\\n    # Read log information (similar to original _build_context)\\n    last_log_time: Optional[datetime] = None\\n    total_entries = 0\\n    minutes_since_log: Optional[float] = None\\n\\n    try:\\n        from scribe_mcp.utils.logs import read_all_lines, parse_log_line\\n        lines = await read_all_lines(log_path)\\n\\n        for line in lines:\\n            if parse_log_line(line):\\n                total_entries += 1\\n\\n        for line in reversed(lines):\\n            parsed = parse_log_line(line)\\n            if not parsed:\\n                continue\\n            ts_str = parsed.get(\\\"ts\\\")\\n            if ts_str:\\n                try:\\n                    from scribe_mcp.utils.time import parse_utc, utcnow\\n                    last_log_time = parse_utc(ts_str)\\n                    delta = utcnow() - last_log_time\\n                    minutes_since_log = delta.total_seconds() / 60\\n                    break\\n                except Exception:\\n                    pass\\n    except Exception:\\n        # If we can't read logs, use defaults\\n        pass\\n\\n    # Extract docs information\\n    docs_status = {}\\n    docs_changed = []\\n\\n    try:\\n        if state:\\n            # Try to get docs information from state\\n            if hasattr(state, 'projects') and project_name in state.projects:\\n                state_project = state.projects[project_name]\\n                docs_status = state_project.get(\\\"docs_status\\\", {})\\n                docs_changed = state_project.get(\\\"docs_changed\\\", [])\\n\\n        # Fall back to checking docs directly\\n        if not docs_status and \\\"docs\\\" in project:\\n            docs = project[\\\"docs\\\"] or {}\\n            for doc_type, doc_path in docs.items():\\n                if doc_type == \\\"progress_log\\\":\\n                    continue\\n                path = Path(doc_path)\\n                if not path.exists():\\n                    docs_status[doc_type] = \\\"missing\\\"\\n                else:\\n                    try:\\n                        content = await asyncio.to_thread(path.read_text, encoding=\\\"utf-8\\\")\\n                        # Simple completion check\\n                        if \\\"{{\\\" in content and \\\"}}\\\" in content:\\n                            docs_status[doc_type] = \\\"incomplete\\\"\\n                        elif len(content.strip()) < 400:\\n                            docs_status[doc_type] = \\\"incomplete\\\"\\n                        else:\\n                            docs_status[doc_type] = \\\"complete\\\"\\n                    except Exception:\\n                        docs_status[doc_type] = \\\"missing\\\"\\n\\n    except Exception:\\n        # If we can't check docs, use empty status\\n        pass\\n\\n    # Get current phase\\n    current_phase = None\\n    try:\\n        phase_plan_path = project.get(\\\"docs\\\", {}).get(\\\"phase_plan\\\")\\n        if phase_plan_path:\\n            phase_path = Path(phase_plan_path)\\n            if phase_path.exists():\\n                import re\\n                content = await asyncio.to_thread(phase_path.read_text, encoding=\\\"utf-8\\\")\\n                match = re.search(r\\\"##\\\\s+Phase\\\\s+(.+?)\\\\s*\\\\(In Progress\\\\)\\\", content)\\n                if match:\\n                    current_phase = match.group(1).strip()\\n    except Exception:\\n        pass\\n\\n    # Get session information\\n    session_age_minutes = None\\n    try:\\n        if state and hasattr(state, 'session_started_at') and state.session_started_at:\\n            from scribe_mcp.utils.time import parse_utc, utcnow\\n            start_dt = parse_utc(state.session_started_at)\\n            if start_dt:\\n                age_delta = utcnow() - start_dt\\n                session_age_minutes = age_delta.total_seconds() / 60\\n    except Exception:\\n        pass\\n\\n    return NewReminderContext(\\n        tool_name=tool_name,\\n        project_name=project_name,\\n        project_root=str(project_root) if project_root else None,\\n        agent_id=agent_id,\\n        total_entries=total_entries,\\n        minutes_since_log=minutes_since_log,\\n        last_log_time=last_log_time,\\n        docs_status=docs_status,\\n        docs_changed=docs_changed,\\n        current_phase=current_phase,\\n\", \"line_end\": 200, \"line_start\": 1}], \"frontmatter\": {}, \"frontmatter_byte_count\": 0, \"frontmatter_line_count\": 0, \"frontmatter_raw\": \"\", \"has_frontmatter\": false, \"mode\": \"chunk\", \"ok\": true, \"reminders\": [{\"category\": \"context\", \"context\": \"Entries:    Last: reset_reminder_cooldowns(*, project_root: str, agent_id: Optional[str] = None) -> int:\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Clear reminder cooldown timestamps for a repo/project (and optionally agent).\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    engine = _get_engine()\\n    return engine.reset_cooldowns(project_root=project_root, agent_id=agent_id)\\n\\n\\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\\n# Legacy Compatibility API\\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\\n\\nasync def get_reminders(\\n    project: Dict[str, Any],\\n    *,\\n    tool_name: str,\\n    state: Optional[object] = None,\\n    agent_id: Optional[str] = None,\\n    variables: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = None,\\n) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    Legacy compatibility wrapper for the original get_reminders function.\\n\\n    This maintains the exact same interface as the original while using\\n    the new reminder engine under the hood.\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    if not project:\\n        return []\\n\\n    # Build the new reminder context from the old format\\n    context = await _build_legacy_context(\\n        project,\\n        tool_name,\\n        state,\\n        agent_id=agent_id,\\n        variables=variables,\\n    )\\n\\n    # Use the new engine\\n    engine = _get_engine()\\n    reminder_instances = await engine.generate_reminders(context)\\n\\n    # Convert to the old format\\n    return engine.to_dict_list(reminder_instances)\\n\\n\\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\\n# Context Building Functions\\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\\n\\nasync def _build_legacy_context(\\n    project: Dict[str, Any],\\n    tool_name: str,\\n    state: Optional[object],\\n    *,\\n    agent_id: Optional[str] = None,\\n    variables: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = None,\\n) -> NewReminderContext:\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Convert legacy project/state format to new ReminderContext.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\n    # Extract project information\\n    project_name = project.get(\\\"name\\\")\\n    project_root = project.get(\\\"root\\\")\\n    log_path = Path(project.get(\\\"progress_log\\\", \\\"\\\"))\\n\\n    # Read log information (similar to original _build_context)\\n    last_log_time: Optional[datetime] = None\\n    total_entries = 0\\n    minutes_since_log: Optional[float] = None\\n\\n    try:\\n        from scribe_mcp.utils.logs import read_all_lines, parse_log_line\\n        lines = await read_all_lines(log_path)\\n\\n        for line in lines:\\n            if parse_log_line(line):\\n                total_entries += 1\\n\\n        for line in reversed(lines):\\n            parsed = parse_log_line(line)\\n            if not parsed:\\n                continue\\n            ts_str = parsed.get(\\\"ts\\\")\\n            if ts_str:\\n                try:\\n                    from scribe_mcp.utils.time import parse_utc, utcnow\\n                    last_log_time = parse_utc(ts_str)\\n                    delta = utcnow() - last_log_time\\n                    minutes_since_log = delta.total_seconds() / 60\\n                    break\\n                except Exception:\\n                    pass\\n    except Exception:\\n        # If we can't read logs, use defaults\\n        pass\\n\\n    # Extract docs information\\n    docs_status = {}\\n    docs_changed = []\\n\\n    try:\\n        if state:\\n            # Try to get docs information from state\\n            if hasattr(state, 'projects') and project_name in state.projects:\\n                state_project = state.projects[project_name]\\n                docs_status = state_project.get(\\\"docs_status\\\", {})\\n                docs_changed = state_project.get(\\\"docs_changed\\\", [])\\n\\n        # Fall back to checking docs directly\\n        if not docs_status and \\\"docs\\\" in project:\\n            docs = project[\\\"docs\\\"] or {}\\n            for doc_type, doc_path in docs.items():\\n                if doc_type == \\\"progress_log\\\":\\n                    continue\\n                path = Path(doc_path)\\n                if not path.exists():\\n                    docs_status[doc_type] = \\\"missing\\\"\\n                else:\\n                    try:\\n                        content = await asyncio.to_thread(path.read_text, encoding=\\\"utf-8\\\")\\n                        # Simple completion check\\n                        if \\\"{{\\\" in content and \\\"}}\\\" in content:\\n                            docs_status[doc_type] = \\\"incomplete\\\"\\n                        elif len(content.strip()) < 400:\\n                            docs_status[doc_type] = \\\"incomplete\\\"\\n                        else:\\n                            docs_status[doc_type] = \\\"complete\\\"\\n                    except Exception:\\n                        docs_status[doc_type] = \\\"missing\\\"\\n\\n    except Exception:\\n        # If we can't check docs, use empty status\\n        pass\\n\\n    # Get current phase\\n    current_phase = None\\n    try:\\n        phase_plan_path = project.get(\\\"docs\\\", {}).get(\\\"phase_plan\\\")\\n        if phase_plan_path:\\n            phase_path = Path(phase_plan_path)\\n            if phase_path.exists():\\n session_age_minutes = None\\n    try:\\n        if state and hasattr(state, 'session_started_at') and state.session_started_at:\\n            from scribe_mcp.utils.time import parse_utc, utcnow\\n            start_dt = parse_utc(state.session_started_at)\\n            if start_dt:\\n                age_delta = utcnow() - start_dt\\n                session_age_minutes = age_delta.total_seconds() / 60\\n    except Exception:\\n        pass\\n\\n    return NewReminderContext(\\n        tool_name=tool_name,\\n        project_name=project_name,\\n        project_root=str(project_root) if project_root else None,\\n        agent_id=agent_id,\\n        total_entries=total_entries,\\n        minutes_since_log=minutes_since_log,\\n        last_log_time=last_log_time,\\n agent_id -> (session_id, expires_at)\n        self._lease_lock = asyncio.Lock()\n        self._session_ttl_minutes = 15  # 15 minute session leases\n\n    async def start_session(self, agent_id: str, metadata: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = None) -> str:\n        \"\"\"\n        Start a new agent session.\n\n        Args:\n            agent_id: Unique identifier for the agent\n            metadata: Optional session metadata\n\n        Returns:\n            Session ID for tracking\n        \"\"\"\n        session_id = str(uuid.uuid4())\n\n        # Store session in database\n        await self.storage.upsert_agent_session(agent_id, session_id, metadata)\n\n        # Cache session lease\n        expires_at = utcnow() + timedelta(minutes=self._session_ttl_minutes)\n        async with self._lease_lock:\n            self._session_leases[agent_id] = (session_id, expires_at)\n\n        # Log session start\n        await self.log_agent_event(\n            agent_id=agent_id,\n            session_id=session_id,\n            event_type=\"session_started\",\n            to_project=\"\",  # No project yet\n            metadata={\"session_ttl_minutes\": self._session_ttl_minutes, \"metadata\": metadata}\n        )\n\n        # Mirror to JSON state for UI continuity\n        await self._mirror_session_to_json_state(agent_id, session_id, metadata)\n\n        return session_id\n\n    async def set_current_project(\n        self,\n        agent_id: str,\n        project_name: Optional[str],\n        session_id: str,\n        expected_version: Optional[int] = None\n    ) -> Dict[str, Any]:\n        \"\"\"\n        Set the current project for an agent with optimistic concurrency control.\n\n        Args:\n            agent_id: Agent identifier\n            project_name: Project name (None to clear)\n            session_id: Valid session ID\n            expected_version: Expected version for optimistic locking\n\n        Returns:\n            Updated agent project record\n\n        Raises:\n            SessionLeaseExpired: If session lease is expired\n            ConflictError: If version conflict occurs\n        \"\"\"\n        # Validate session lease\n        await self._validate_session_lease(agent_id, session_id)\n\n        # Get previous project for audit logging\n        previous_project = None\n        try:\n            current = await self.storage.get_agent_project(agent_id)\n            if current and current.get(\"project_name\"):\n                previous_project = current[\"project_name\"]\n        except Exception:\n            pass\n\n        # Set in database (source of truth)\n        try:\n            result = await self.storage.set_agent_project(\n                agent_id=agent_id,\n                project_name=project_name,\n                expected_version=expected_version,\n                updated_by=agent_id,\n                session_id=session_id\n            )\n\n            # Log successful project change\n            event_type = \"project_switched\" if previous_project and previous_project != project_name else \"project_set\"\n            await self.log_agent_event(\n                agent_id=agent_id,\n                session_id=session_id,\n                event_type=event_type,\n                from_project=previous_project,\n                to_project=project_name,\n                expected_version=expected_version,\n                actual_version=result[\"version\"] if hasattr(result, \"__getitem__\") else result.get(\"version\"),\n                success=True,\n                metadata={\"updated_by\": agent_id}\n            )\n\n            # Mirror to JSON state (non-authoritative cache)\n            await self._mirror_project_to_json_state(agent_id, result)\n\n            return result\n\n        except Exception as e:\n            # Log failed project change\n            await self.log_agent_event(\n                agent_id=agent_id,\n                session_id=session_id,\n                event_type=\"conflict_detected\",\n                from_project=previous_project,\n                to_project=project_name,\n                expected_version=expected_version,\n                success=False,\n                error_message=str(e),\n                metadata={\"error_type\": type(e).__name__}\n            )\n            raise\n\n    async def get_current_project(self, agent_id: str) -> Optional[Dict[str, Any]]:\n        \"\"\"\n        Get an agent's current project from database (source of truth).\n\n        Args:\n            agent_id: Agent identifier\n\n        Returns:\n            Agent project record or None\n        \"\"\"\n        return await self.storage.get_agent_project(agent_id)\n\n    async def heartbeat_session(self, session_id: str) -> None:\n        \"\"\"\n        Update session activity timestamp.\n\n        Args:\n            session_id: Session ID to update\n        \"\"\"\n        await self.storage.heartbeat_session(session_id)\n\n        # Update local lease cache\n        async with self._lease_lock:\n            for agent_id, (cached_session_id, expires_at) in self._session_leases.items():\n                if cached_session_id == session_id:\n                    # Extend lease\n                    new_expires_at = utcnow() + timedelta(minutes=self._session_ttl_minutes)\n                    self._session_leases[agent_id] = (session_id, new_expires_at)\n                    break\n\n    async def end_session(self, agent_id: str, session_id: str) -> None:\n        \"\"\"\n        End an agent session.\n\n        Args:\n            agent_id: Agent identifier\n            session_id: Session ID to end\n        \"\"\"\n        # Get current project for audit logging\n        current_project = None\n        try:\n            project = await self.storage.get_agent_project(agent_id)\n            if project and project.get(\"project_name\"):\n                current_project = project[\"project_name\"]\n        except Exception:\n            pass\n\n        # Mark session as expired in database\n        await self.storage.end_session(session_id)\n", "line_end": 200, "line_start": 1}], "frontmatter": {}, "frontmatter_byte_count": 0, "frontmatter_line_count": 0, "frontmatter_raw": "", "has_frontmatter": false, "mode": "chunk", "ok": true, "reminders": [{"category": "context", "context": "Entries:    Last: ", "emoji": "\ud83c\udfaf", "level": "info", "message": "Project: scribe_sentinel_concurrency_v1", "score": 3, "tone": "neutral"}], "scan": {"absolute_path": "/home/austin/projects/MCP_SPINE/scribe_mcp/state/agent_manager.py", "byte_size": 18508, "encoding": "utf-8", "estimated_chunk_count": 3, "line_count": 505, "newline_type": "LF", "repo_relative_path": "state/agent_manager.py", "sha256": "9a4e06d07f3481eb44ba98815854b8cd89125e41b54f2e50c218bf2f0842bf7b"}}; timestamp=2026-01-03T06:56:41.819025Z; tool=read_file; priority=medium; log_type=tool_logs; content_type=log

return cleaned_count\n\n    async def _validate_session_lease(self, agent_id: str, session_id: str) -> None:\n        \"\"\"\n        Validate that a session lease is still active.\n\n        Args:\n with self._lease_lock:\n            if agent_id not in self._session_leases:\n                raise SessionLeaseExpired(f\"No active session for agent {agent_id}\")\n\n            cached_session_id, expires_at = self._session_leases[agent_id]\n            if cached_session_id != session_id:\n                raise SessionLeaseExpired(f\"Session ID mismatch for agent {agent_id}\")\n\n            if expires_at < utcnow():\n                raise SessionLeaseExpired(f\"Session lease expired for agent {agent_id}\")\n\n    async def _mirror_session_to_json_state(self, agent_id: str, session_id: str, metadata: Optional[Dict[str, Any]]) -> None:\n        \"\"\"\n        Mirror session information to JSON state for UI continuity.\n\n        Args:\n            agent_id: Agent identifier\n            session_id: Session ID\n            metadata: Session metadata\n        \"\"\"\n        # Create minimal crumbs in JSON state (non-authoritative)\n        state = await self.state_manager.load()\n\n        # We could add agent sessions to JSON state if needed for UI\n        # For now, just update last activity and agent tracking\n        await self.state_manager.persist(state)\n\n    async def _mirror_project_to_json_state(self, agent_id: str, agent_project: Dict[str, Any]) -> None:\n        \"\"\"\n        Mirror agent project to JSON state for UI continuity.\n\n        Args:\n            agent_id: Agent identifier\n            agent_project: Agent project record\n        \"\"\"\n        # Update JSON state with minimal information for UI continuity\n        state = await self.state_manager.load()\n\n        # Note: JSON state is now just a cache/warm-start mechanism\n        # The authoritative source of truth is the database\n\n        # Update version, timestamp, and tracking info\n        # This keeps the existing UI working while transitioning to agent-scoped context\n\n    async def log_agent_event(\n        self,\n        agent_id: str,\n        session_id: str,\n        event_type: str,\n        to_project: str,\n        from_project: Optional[str] = None,\n        expected_version: Optional[int] = None,\n        actual_version: agent project event to the audit trail.\n\n        Args:\n            agent_id: Agent identifier\n            session_id: Session identifier\n            event_type: Type of event ('project_set', 'project_switched', 'conflict_detected', etc.)\n            to_project: Target project name\n            from_project: Source project name (for switches)\n            expected_version: Expected version for optimistic concurrency\n            actual_version: Actual version after operation\n            success: Whether the operation succeeded\n            error_message: Error message if operation failed\n            metadata: Additional event metadata\n        \"\"\"\n        try:\n            import json\n\n            event_data = {\n                \"agent_id\": agent_id,\n                \"session_id\": session_id,\n                \"event_type\": event_type,\n                \"from_project\": from_project,\n                \"to_project\": to_project,\n                \"expected_version\": expected_version,\n                \"actual_version\": actual_version,\n                \"success\": success,\n                \"error_message\": error_message,\n                \"metadata\": json.dumps(metadata or {}),\n                \"created_at\": utcnow().isoformat(),\n            }\n\n            # Store in database if available\n            try:\n                # Use the storage backend's execute method if available\n                if hasattr(self.storage, '_execute'):\n                    query = \"\"\"\n                        INSERT INTO agent_project_events                        ) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)\n                    \"\"\"\n                    await self.storage._execute(\n                        query,\n                        (\n                            agent_id, session_id, event_type, from_project, to_project,\n                            expected_version, actual_version, success, error_message,\n                            event_data[\"metadata\"], event_data[\"created_at\"]\n                        )\n                    )\n            except Exception as db_error:\n                # Database audit logging logging fails\n            print(f\"Warning: Failed to log agent event: {e}\")\n\n    async def get_agent_events(\n        self,\n        agent_id: Optional[str] = None,\n        event_type:    \"\"\"\n        try:\n            where_clauses = []\n            params = []\n\n            if agent_id:\n                where_clauses.append(\"agent_id = ?\")\n [ℹ️] [2026-01-03 06:56:58 UTC] [Agent: Scribe] [Project: scribe_sentinel_concurrency_v1] Tool call: read_file | format_requested=readable; response_data={"chunks": [{"byte_end": 21586, "byte_start": 14117, "chunk_index": 2, "content": "        query = f\"\"\"\n            SELECT\n                p.name AS project_slug,\n                p.name AS project_name,\n                p.description,\n                COALESCE(p.status, 'planning') AS status,\n                p.created_at,\n                p.last_entry_at,\n                p.last_access_at,\n                p.last_status_change,\n                COALESCE(m.total_entries, 0) AS total_entries,\n                COALESCE(df.total_files, 0) AS total_files,\n                COALESCE(ph.total_phases, 0) AS total_phases,\n                p.tags,\n                p.meta\n            FROM scribe_projects p\n            LEFT JOIN scribe_metrics m\n                ON m.project_id = p.id\n            LEFT JOIN (\n                SELECT project_id, COUNT(*) AS total_files\n                FROM dev_plans\n                GROUP BY project_id\n            ) AS df\n                ON df.project_id = p.id\n            LEFT JOIN (\n                SELECT project_id, COUNT(*) AS total_phases\n                FROM phases\n                GROUP BY project_id\n            ) AS ph\n                ON ph.project_id = p.id\n            WHERE {where_clause}\n            ORDER BY COALESCE(p.last_access_at, p.last_entry_at, p.created_at) DESC\n            LIMIT 1\n        \"\"\"\n\n        with sqlite3.connect(self._db_path) as conn:\n            conn.row_factory = sqlite3.Row\n            row = conn.execute(query, params).fetchone()\n\n        if not row:\n            return None\n        return self._row_to_project_info(row)\n\n    # ------------------------------------------------------------------\n    # Internal helpers\n    # ------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n    def _ensure_schema(self) -> None:\n        \"\"\"Ensure registry-specific columns exist on scribe_projects.\n\n        We only add columns that are safe no-ops on existing installs.\n        Older fields like status/phase/last_activity are preserved.\n        \"\"\"\n        with sqlite3.connect(self._db_path) as conn:\n            cursor = conn.cursor()\n            # Create a minimal table if it does not exist. This avoids first-run\n            # failures when running Scribe against a fresh repo before the main\n            # storage backend has created schema.\n            cursor.execute(\n                \"\"\"\n                CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS scribe_projects (\n                    id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,\n                    name TEXT UNIQUE,\n                    repo_root TEXT,\n                    progress_log_path TEXT,\n                    description TEXT,\n                    status TEXT,\n                    progress_log TEXT,\n                    root TEXT,\n                    created_at TEXT,\n                    last_entry_at TEXT,\n                    last_access_at TEXT,\n                    last_status_change TEXT,\n                    tags TEXT,\n                    meta TEXT\n                )\n                \"\"\"\n            )\n\n            cursor.execute(\"PRAGMA table_info(scribe_projects)\")\n            existing = {row[1] for row in cursor.fetchall()}\n\n            def add_column(name: str, ddl: str) -> None:\n                if name in existing:\n                    return\n                cursor.execute(f\"ALTER TABLE scribe_projects ADD COLUMN {ddl}\")\n\n            # New registry-focused fields for v1\n            add_column(\"description\", \"description TEXT\")\n            add_column(\"last_entry_at\", \"last_entry_at TEXT\")\n            add_column(\"last_access_at\", \"last_access_at TEXT\")\n            add_column(\"last_status_change\", \"last_status_change TEXT\")\n            add_column(\"tags\", \"tags TEXT\")\n            add_column(\"meta\", \"meta TEXT\")\n            conn.commit()\n\n    @staticmethod\n    def _now_iso() -> str:\n        return datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat()\n\n    @staticmethod\n    def _parse_ts(value: Optional[str]) -> Optional[datetime]:\n        if not value:\n            return None\n        try:\n            dt = datetime.fromisoformat(value)\n            if dt.tzinfo is None:\n                return dt.replace(tzinfo=timezone.utc)\n            return dt.astimezone(timezone.utc)\n        except ValueError:\n            return None\n\n    def _row_to_project_info(self, row: sqlite3.Row) -> ProjectInfo:\n        tags_raw = row[\"tags\"]\n        tags: List[str] = []\n        if tags_raw:\n            tags = [t for t in tags_raw.split(\",\") if t]\n\n        meta_raw = row[\"meta\"]\n        meta: Dict[str, Any] = {}\n        if meta_raw:\n            try:\n                import json\n\n                meta = json.loads(meta_raw)\n            except Exception:\n                meta = {\"raw\": meta_raw}\n\n        # Ensure project-scoped metadata container exists and has a work_blockers list.\n        project_meta = (meta.get(\"project\") or {}).copy()\n        project_meta.setdefault(\"work_blockers\", [])\n        meta[\"project\"] = project_meta\n\n        # Derive activity metrics for this project (non-persistent).\n        now = datetime.now(timezone.utc)\n        created_ts = self._parse_ts(row[\"created_at\"]) or now\n        last_entry_ts = self._parse_ts(row[\"last_entry_at\"]) or created_ts\n        last_access_ts = self._parse_ts(row[\"last_access_at\"]) or created_ts\n\n        age_days = max(0.0, (now - created_ts).total_seconds() / 86400.0)\n        since_entry = max(0.0, (now - last_entry_ts).total_seconds() / 86400.0)\n        since_access = max(0.0, (now - last_access_ts).total_seconds() / 86400.0)\n\n        if age_days <= 2:\n            staleness_level = \"fresh\"\n        elif age_days <= 7:\n            staleness_level = \"warming\"\n        elif age_days <= 30:\n            staleness_level = \"stale\"\n        else:\n            staleness_level = \"frozen\"\n\n        # Basic activity metrics.\n        activity_meta: Dict[str, Any] = {\n            \"project_age_days\": age_days,\n            \"days_since_last_entry\": since_entry,\n            \"days_since_last_access\": since_access,\n            \"staleness_level\": staleness_level,\n        }\n\n        # Activity score: higher means \"more active / higher priority\".\n        entries = int(row[\"total_entries\"])\n        entry_rate = entries / age_days if age_days > 0 else float(entries)\n\n        priority_raw = project_meta.get(\"priority\")\n        priority_score = 0.0\n        if isinstance(priority_raw, (int, float)):\n            priority_score = float(priority_raw)\n        elif isinstance(priority_raw, str):\n            _prio_map = {\n                \"low\": 0.0,\n                \"medium\": 1.0,\n                \"high\": 2.0,\n                \"critical\": 3.0,\n            }\n            priority_score = _prio_map.get(priority_raw.lower(), 0.0)\n\n        # Doc flags may influence activity score (e.g., docs ready for work).\n        docs_meta = (meta.get(\"docs\") or {}).copy()\n        flags = (docs_meta.get(\"flags\") or {}).copy()\n        docs_ready = bool(flags.get(\"docs_ready_for_work\"))\n\n        activity_score = (\n            -since_entry\n            - 0.5 * since_access\n            + 1.5 * entry_rate\n            + (2.0 if docs_ready else 0.0)\n            + 0.5 * priority_score\n        )\n        activity_meta[\"activity_score\"] = activity_score\n\n        # Doc drift hints based on docs meta + lifecycle.\n        status = row[\"status\"]\n        last_docs_update_ts = None\n        last_docs_update_raw = docs_meta.get(\"last_update_at\")\n        if isinstance(last_docs_update_raw, str):\n            last_docs_update_ts = self._parse_ts(last_docs_update_raw)\n\n        doc_drift = False\n        doc_drift_days = None\n", "line_end": 600, "line_start": 401}], "frontmatter": {}, "frontmatter_byte_count": 0, "frontmatter_line_count": 0, "frontmatter_raw": "", "has_frontmatter": false, "mode": "chunk", "ok": true, "reminders": [{"category": "context", "context": "Entries:    Last: ", "emoji": "\ud83c\udfaf", "level": "info", "message": "Project: scribe_sentinel_concurrency_v1", "score": 3, "tone": "neutral"}], "scan": {"absolute_path": "/home/austin/projects/MCP_SPINE/scribe_mcp/shared/project_registry.py", "byte_size": 23401, "encoding": "utf-8", "estimated_chunk_count": 4, "line_count": 648, "newline_type": "LF", "repo_relative_path": "shared/project_registry.py", "sha256": "4a2a0bfe8f6d1d5a05398e4f9543750e74453a8d091ffa35fb02d2d71ab416fd"}}; timestamp=2026-01-03T06:56:58.128177Z; tool=read_file; priority=medium; log_type=tool_logs; content_type=log

Optional for some backends.\"\"\"\n\n    async def close(self) -> None:\n        \"\"\"Release held resources.\"\"\"\n\n    @abstractmethod\n    async def upsert_project(\n        self,\n        *,\n        name: str,\n        repo_root: str,\n        progress_log_path: str,\n    ) -> ProjectRecord:\n        \"\"\"Insert or update a project row and return the record.\"\"\"\n\n    @abstractmethod\n    async def fetch_project(self, name: str) -> Optional[ProjectRecord]:\n        \"\"\"Return the project by name when present.\"\"\"\n\n    @abstractmethod\n    async def list_projects(self) -> List[ProjectRecord]:\n        \"\"\"Return all known projects.\"\"\"\n\n    @abstractmethod\n    async def delete_project(self, name: str) -> bool:\n        \"\"\"Delete a project and all associated data. \"\"\"Return recent entries for the given project.\"\"\"\n\n    async def fetch_recent_entries_paginated(\n        self,\n        *,\n        project: ProjectRecord,\n        page: int = 1,\n        page_size: int = 50,\n        filters: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = None,\n    ) -> Tuple[List[Dict[str, Any]], int]:\n        \"\"\"\n        Return recent entries with pagination metadata.\n\n        Args:\n            project: Project to fetch entries for\n            page: Page number (1-based)\n            page_size: Number of entries per page\n            filters: Optional filters to apply\n\n        Returns:\n            Tuple of (entries, total_count)\n        \"\"\"\n        # Calculate offset\n        offset = (page - 1) * page_size\n\n        # Fetch entries for this page\n        entries = await self.fetch_recent_entries(\n            project=project,\n            limit=page_size,\n            filters=filters,\n            offset=offset\n        )\n\n        # Get total count (this varies by backend implementation)\n        total_count = await self.count_entries(project, filters)\n\n        return entries, total_count\n\n    async def count_entries(\n        self,\n        project: ProjectRecord,\n        filters: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = None,\n    ) -> int:\n        \"\"\"\n        Count total entries matching filters.\n        Default implementation - can be "chunk_index": 0, "content": "\"\"\"SQLite storage backend (default).\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport asyncio\nimport json\nimport sqlite3\nfrom datetime import datetime\nfrom pathlib import Path\nfrom typing import Any, Dict, List, Optional\n\nfrom scribe_mcp.storage.base import StorageBackend\nfrom scribe_mcp.storage.models import (\n    ProjectRecord, DevPlanRecord, PhaseRecord, MilestoneRecord,\n    BenchmarkRecord, ChecklistRecord, PerformanceMetricsRecord,\n    DocumentSectionRecord, CustomTemplateRecord, DocumentChangeRecord, SyncStatusRecord\n)\nfrom scribe_mcp.utils.time import format_utc, utcnow\nfrom scribe_mcp.utils.search import message_matches\n\nSQLITE_TIMEOUT_SECONDS = 30\nSQLITE_BUSY_TIMEOUT_MS = 5000\n\n\nclass SQLiteStorage(StorageBackend):\n    \"\"\"SQLite-backed persistence with lazy connections.\"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self, db_path: Path   str) -> None:\n        self._path = Path(db_path).expanduser()\n        self._init_lock = asyncio.Lock()\n        self._write_lock = asyncio.Lock()\n        self._initialised = False\n\n    async def setup(self) -> None:\n        await self._initialise()\n\n    async def close(self) -> None:  # pragma: no cover - nothing persistent to close\n        # Connections are short-lived; nothing to do.\n        return None\n\n    async def upsert_project(\n        self,\n        *,\n        name: str,\n        repo_root: str,\n        progress_log_path: str,\n    ) -> ProjectRecord:\n        await self._initialise()\n        async with self._write_lock:\n            await self._execute(\n            \"\"\"\n            INSERT INTO scribe_projects (name, repo_root, progress_log_path)\n            VALUES (?, ?, ?)\n            ON CONFLICT(name)\n            DO UPDATE SET repo_root = excluded.repo_root,\n                          progress_log_path = excluded.progress_log_path;\n            \"\"\",\n            (name, repo_root, progress_log_path),\n        )\n        row = await self._fetchone(\n            \"\"\"\n            SELECT id, name, repo_root, progress_log_path\n            FROM scribe_projects\n            WHERE name = ?;\n            \"\"\",\n            (name,),\n        )\n        return ProjectRecord(\n            id=row[\"id\"],\n            name=row[\"name\"],\n   async def delete_project(self, name: str) -> bool:\n        \"\"\"Delete a project and all associated data with proper cascade handling.\"\"\"\n        await self._initialise()\n\n        # First Agent session and project context management methods\n    async def upsert_agent_session(self, agent_id: str, session_id: str, metadata: Optional[Dict[str, Any]]) -> None:\n        \"\"\"Create or update an agent session.\"\"\"\n        await self._initialise()\n        metadata_json = json.dumps(metadata or {}) if metadata else None\n        async with self._write_lock:\n            await self._execute(\n                \"\"\"\n                INSERT INTO agent_sessions (id, agent_id, started_at, last_active_at, status, metadata)\n                VALUES (?, ?, CURRENT_TIMESTAMP, CURRENT_TIMESTAMP, 'active', ?)\n                ON CONFLICT(id) DO UPDATE SET\n                    last_active_at = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,\n                    status = 'active',\n                    metadata = excluded.metadata;\n                \"\"\",\n                (session_id, agent_id, metadata_json)\n            )\n\n    async def heartbeat_session(self, session_id: str) -> None:\n        \"\"\"Update session last_active_at timestamp.\"\"\"\n        await self._initialise()\n        async with self._write_lock:\n            await self._execute(\n                \"\"\"\n                UPDATE agent_sessions\n                SET last_active_at = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP\n                WHERE id = ? AND status = 'active';\n                \"\"\",\n                (session_id,)\n            )\n\n    async def end_session(self, session_id: str) -> None:\n        \"\"\"Mark a session as expired.\"\"\"\n        await self._initialise()\n        async with self._write_lock:\n            await self._execute(\n                \"\"\"\n                UPDATE agent_sessions\n                SET status = 'expired', last_active_at = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP\n                WHERE id = ?;\n                \"\"\",\n                (session_id,)\n            )\n\n    async def get_agent_project(self, agent_id: str) -> Optional[Dict[str, Any]]:\n        \"\"\"Get an agent's current project with version info.\"\"\"\n        await self._initialise()\n        row = await self._fetchone(\n            \"\"\"\n            SELECT agent_id, project_name, version, updated_at, updated_by, session_id\n            FROM agent_projects\n            WHERE agent_id = ?;\n            \"\"\",\n            (agent_id,)\n        )\n        if not row:\n            return None\n\n        return {\n            \"agent_id\": row[\"agent_id\"],\n            \"project_name\": row[\"project_name\"],\n            \"version\": row[\"version\"],\n            \"updated_at\": row[\"updated_at\"],\n            \"updated_by\": row[\"updated_by\"],\n            \"session_id\": row[\"session_id\"]\n        }\n\n    async def set_agent_project(self, agent_id: str, project_name: Optional[str], expected_version: Optional[int], updated_by: str, session_id: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:\n        \"\"\"Set an agent's current project with optimistic concurrency control.\"\"\"\n        await self._initialise()\n        async with self._write_lock:\n            if expected_version is not None:\n                # Optimistic concurrency check\n                cursor = await self._fetchone(\n                    \"\"\"\n                    UPDATE agent_projects\n                    SET project_name = ?, version = version + 1, updated_at = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,\n                        updated_by = ?, session_id = ?\n                    WHERE agent_id = ? AND version = ?\n                    RETURNING agent_id, project_name, version, updated_at, updated_by, session_id;\n                    \"\"\",\n                    (project_name, updated_by, session_id, agent_id, expected_version)\n                )\n                if not cursor:\n                    from scribe_mcp.storage.base import ConflictError\n                    raise ConflictError(f\"Version conflict for agent {agent_id}: expected version {expected_version}\")\n                result = cursor\n            else:\n                # First time or no version check\n                await self._execute(\n                    \"\"\"\n                    INSERT INTO agent_projects (agent_id, project_name, version, updated_by, session_id)\n                    VALUES (?, ?, 1, ?, ?)\n                    ON CONFLICT(agent_id) DO UPDATE SET\n                        project_name = excluded.project_name,\n                        version = version + 1,\n                        updated_at = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,\n                        updated_by = excluded.updated_by,\n                        session_id = excluded.session_id;\n                    \"\"\",\n                    (agent_id, project_name, updated_by, session_id)\n                )\n                result = await self.get_agent_project(agent_id)\n\n            return result or await self.get_agent_project(agent_id)\n", "line_end": 1524, "line_start": 1401}], "frontmatter": {}, "frontmatter_byte_count": 0, "frontmatter_line_count": 0, "frontmatter_raw": "", "has_frontmatter": false, "mode": "chunk", "ok": true, "reminders": [{"category": "context", "context": "Entries:    Last: ", "emoji": "\ud83c\udfaf", "level": "info", "message": "Project: scribe_sentinel_concurrency_v1", "score": 3, "tone": "neutral"}], "scan": {"absolute_path": "/home/austin/projects/MCP_SPINE/scribe_mcp/storage/sqlite.py", "byte_size": 66434, "encoding": "utf-8", "estimated_chunk_count": 8, "line_count": 1524, "newline_type": "LF", "repo_relative_path": "storage/sqlite.py", "sha256": "15a8cbe6989123d01e177f10175a15b6f3b9458707ecedff6354ad56de0f39d2"}}; timestamp=2026-01-03T06:58:58.444057Z; tool=read_file; priority=medium; log_type=tool_logs; content_type=log

Optimistic concurrency check\\n                cursor = await self._fetchone(\\n                    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n                    UPDATE agent_projects\\n                    SET project_name = ?, version = version + 1, updated_at = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,\\n                        updated_by = ?, session_id = ?\\n                    WHERE agent_id = ? version check\\n                await self._execute(\\n                    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n                    INSERT INTO agent_projects (agent_id, project_name, version, updated_by, session_id)\\n             \\\"\\\"\\\",\\n                    (agent_id, project_name, updated_by, session_id)\\n                )\\n                result = await self.get_agent_project(agent_id)\\n\\n
